0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CemKVjJug3QGHyV6jDuFF","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_0156Yrwi6ynkdT5RJo3G9gBG","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/home/llmtest/evals/20260901-162328/qtclustering_claude-opus-5-cc-medium_mpi_r3/qtclustering/qtclustering.cpp","content":"// QT Clustering Benchmark - MPI Distributed Memory Version\n//\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy:\n//   * Every rank redundantly generates the (deterministic) input data, so no\n//     input distribution is required.\n//   * In each round of the outer loop, the candidate clusters of the remaining\n//     unclustered seed points are distributed round-robin over the ranks.\n//     Each rank grows the candidate clusters of its own seeds independently.\n//   * A single MPI_Allreduce(MPI_MAXLOC) selects the globally best seed, using\n//     the same tie-breaking rule as the sequential code (largest cardinality,\n//     smallest point index on ties).\n//   * The owner of the winning seed broadcasts the winning cluster members, so\n//     all ranks keep an identical view of the clustering state and no final\n//     gather is required.\n//\n// Serial optimizations that preserve the exact results of the original code:\n//   * Incremental candidate diameters: instead of recomputing the maximum\n//     distance from a candidate to every cluster member, each candidate keeps\n//     a running maximum which is updated with the distance to the newly added\n//     member only (max over a set is order independent, hence bit-identical).\n//   * Candidates whose running maximum reached the threshold are dropped\n//     permanently (the maximum can only grow).\n//   * A uniform grid restricts the initial candidate set of a seed to the\n//     points that are closer than the threshold to that seed - all other\n//     points can never join the cluster since the seed is a cluster member.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Uniform bucket grid over the still unclustered points. It is used to find,\n// for a given seed, all points that are closer than the threshold - only those\n// can ever become members of the seed's candidate cluster.\nstruct NeighborGrid {\n    double cell = 1.0;\n    int nx = 1, ny = 1;\n    int reach = 1;                 // number of cells to search in each direction\n    std::vector<int> cell_start;   // size nx*ny+1\n    std::vector<int> items;        // point indices, ascending within each cell\n    int live_at_build = 0;         // number of points present at last rebuild\n\n    void configure(const double threshold) {\n        // Keep the number of cells bounded for very small thresholds; the\n        // search radius compensates for cells larger than the threshold.\n        cell = std::max(threshold, MAX_WIDTH / 512.0);\n        nx = static_cast<int>(MAX_WIDTH / cell) + 1;\n        ny = static_cast<int>(MAX_HEIGHT / cell) + 1;\n        reach = static_cast<int>(std::ceil(threshold / cell));\n        if (reach < 1) reach = 1;\n        cell_start.assign(static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny + 1, 0);\n    }\n\n    inline int cellX(const Point& p) const {\n        int c = static_cast<int>(p.x / cell);\n        return std::min(std::max(c, 0), nx - 1);\n    }\n    inline int cellY(const Point& p) const {\n        int c = static_cast<int>(p.y / cell);\n        return std::min(std::max(c, 0), ny - 1);\n    }\n\n    // Counting sort of the given point indices into the grid cells.\n    void build(const std::vector<Point>& points, const std::vector<int>& live) {\n        const size_t ncells = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;\n        std::fill(cell_start.begin(), cell_start.end(), 0);\n        for (const int idx : live) {\n            const size_t c = static_cast<size_t>(cellY(points[idx])) * nx + cellX(points[idx]);\n            ++cell_start[c + 1];\n        }\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < ncells; ++c) {\n            cell_start[c + 1] += cell_start[c];\n        }\n        items.resize(live.size());\n        std::vector<int> cursor(cell_start.begin(), cell_start.end() - 1);\n        for (const int idx : live) {\n            const size_t c = static_cast<size_t>(cellY(points[idx])) * nx + cellX(points[idx]);\n            items[cursor[c]++] = idx;\n        }\n        live_at_build = static_cast<int>(live.size());\n    }\n};\n\n// Scratch buffers for growing a candidate cluster (reused across seeds).\nstruct ClusterWorkspace {\n    std::vector<int> idx;      // candidate point indices\n    std::vector<double> px;    // candidate coordinates (kept next to the index\n    std::vector<double> py;    // arrays so the update loop can vectorize)\n    std::vector<double> maxd;  // running max distance to the current members\n    std::vector<int> members;\n\n    void reserve(const int n) {\n        idx.reserve(n);\n        px.reserve(n);\n        py.reserve(n);\n        maxd.reserve(n);\n        members.reserve(n);\n    }\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point.\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster; the members are left in\n// work.members.\nstatic int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                                    const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                                    const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                    const double threshold,\n                                    const NeighborGrid& grid,\n                                    ClusterWorkspace& work) {\n    std::vector<int>& idx = work.idx;\n    std::vector<double>& px = work.px;\n    std::vector<double>& py = work.py;\n    std::vector<double>& maxd = work.maxd;\n    std::vector<int>& members = work.members;\n\n    idx.clear();\n    px.clear();\n    py.clear();\n    maxd.clear();\n    members.clear();\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    const Point seed = points[seed_point];\n\n    // Collect all unclustered points closer than the threshold to the seed.\n    const int cx = grid.cellX(seed);\n    const int cy = grid.cellY(seed);\n    const int x0 = std::max(cx - grid.reach, 0);\n    const int x1 = std::min(cx + grid.reach, grid.nx - 1);\n    const int y0 = std::max(cy - grid.reach, 0);\n    const int y1 = std::min(cy + grid.reach, grid.ny - 1);\n\n    int best = -1;\n    int best_idx = 0;\n    double best_d = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n\n    for (int gy = y0; gy <= y1; ++gy) {\n        const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(gy) * grid.nx;\n        const int begin = grid.cell_start[row + x0];\n        const int end = grid.cell_start[row + x1 + 1];\n        for (int k = begin; k < end; ++k) {\n            const int c = grid.items[k];\n            if (c == seed_point || clustered[c]) continue;\n            const double d = distance(points[c], seed);\n            if (d >= threshold) continue;\n            if (d < best_d || (d == best_d && c < best_idx)) {\n                best = static_cast<int>(idx.size());\n                best_idx = c;\n                best_d = d;\n            }\n            idx.push_back(c);\n            px.push_back(points[c].x);\n            py.push_back(points[c].y);\n            maxd.push_back(d);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Iteratively add the candidate with the smallest cluster diameter.\n    while (best >= 0) {\n        const int added = idx[best];\n        members.push_back(added);\n\n        const double ax = px[best];\n        const double ay = py[best];\n        const int n = static_cast<int>(idx.size());\n\n        // Update the running maxima (branch free, vectorizable).\n        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            const double dx = px[i] - ax;\n            const double dy = py[i] - ay;\n            const double d = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n            maxd[i] = maxd[i] > d ? maxd[i] : d;\n        }\n\n        // Compact out the candidates that can no longer join and pick the next\n        // best one. The newly added member is dropped as well.\n        int out = 0;\n        int nbest = -1;\n        int nbest_idx = 0;\n        double nbest_d = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            if (i == best || maxd[i] >= threshold) continue;\n            const double d = maxd[i];\n            const int c = idx[i];\n            if (d < nbest_d || (d == nbest_d && c < nbest_idx)) {\n                nbest = out;\n                nbest_idx = c;\n                nbest_d = d;\n            }\n            idx[out] = c;\n            px[out] = px[i];\n            py[out] = py[i];\n            maxd[out] = d;\n            ++out;\n        }\n        idx.resize(out);\n        px.resize(out);\n        py.resize(out);\n        maxd.resize(out);\n        best = nbest;\n        best_idx = nbest_idx;\n        best_d = nbest_d;\n    }\n\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (distributed over MPI_COMM_WORLD)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold,\n                                  const int rank,\n                                  const int nranks) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    NeighborGrid grid;\n    grid.configure(threshold);\n    grid.build(points, unclustered_indices);\n\n    ClusterWorkspace work;\n    work.reserve(N);\n\n    std::vector<int> bcast_members(N);\n\n    struct { int card; int seed; } local, global;\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int remaining = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n        // Rebuild the grid once a significant fraction of its points has been\n        // clustered, so that the neighborhood scans stay cheap.\n        if (remaining * 4 <= grid.live_at_build * 3) {\n            grid.build(points, unclustered_indices);\n        }\n\n        local.card = -1;\n        local.seed = N;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n\n        // Try each unclustered point assigned to this rank as a seed.\n        // Round-robin over the (index sorted) unclustered points balances the\n        // very different per-seed costs well and visits the local seeds in\n        // ascending order, which matches the sequential tie-breaking.\n        for (int pos = rank; pos < remaining; pos += nranks) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[pos];\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points,\n                                                             threshold, grid, work);\n            if (cardinality > local.card) {\n                local.card = cardinality;\n                local.seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = work.members;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // MPI_MAXLOC keeps the largest cardinality and, on ties, the smallest\n        // point index - exactly the sequential selection rule.\n        MPI_Allreduce(&local, &global, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (global.seed >= N || global.card <= 0) {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n\n        // The winning seed's owner is determined from its position in the\n        // (globally replicated, ascending) list of unclustered points.\n        const int owner = static_cast<int>(\n            std::lower_bound(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                             global.seed) - unclustered_indices.begin()) % nranks;\n\n        if (rank == owner) {\n            std::copy(best_cluster_members.begin(), best_cluster_members.end(),\n                      bcast_members.begin());\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(bcast_members.data(), global.card, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = global.seed;\n        cluster.members.assign(bcast_members.begin(), bcast_members.begin() + global.card);\n\n        // Mark all members as clustered\n        for (const int m : cluster.members) {\n            clustered[m] = true;\n        }\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n        // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n        unclustered_indices.erase(\n            std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                          [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n            unclustered_indices.end()\n        );\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: 1000)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -t <float>   Distance threshold for clustering (default: 2.0)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0, nranks = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &nranks);\n\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = MPI Distributed Memory Version\n//\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy:\n//   * Every rank redundantly generates the (deterministic) input data, so no\n//     input distribution is required.\n//   * In each round of the outer loop, the candidate clusters of the remaining\n//     unclustered seed points are distributed round-robin over the ranks.\n//     Each rank grows the candidate clusters of its own seeds independently.\n//   * A single MPI_Allreduce(MPI_MAXLOC) selects the globally best seed, using\n//     the same tie-breaking rule as the sequential code (largest cardinality,\n//     smallest point index on ties).\n//   * The owner of the winning seed broadcasts the winning cluster members, so\n//     all ranks keep an identical view of the clustering state and no final\n//     gather is required.\n//\n// Serial optimizations that preserve the exact results of the original code:\n//   * Incremental candidate diameters: instead of recomputing the maximum\n//     distance from a candidate to every cluster member, each candidate keeps\n//     a running maximum which is updated with the distance to the newly added\n//     member only (max over a set is order independent, hence bit-identical).\n//   * Candidates whose running maximum reached the threshold are dropped\n//     permanently (the maximum can only grow).\n//   * A uniform grid restricts the initial candidate set of a seed to the\n//     points that are closer than the threshold to that seed - all other\n//     points can never join the cluster since the seed is a cluster member.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Uniform bucket grid over the still unclustered points. It is used to find,\n// for a given seed, all points that are closer than the threshold - only those\n// can ever become members of the seed's candidate cluster.\nstruct NeighborGrid {\n    double cell = 1.0;\n    int nx = 1, ny = 1;\n    int reach = 1;                 // number of cells to search in each direction\n    std::vector<int> cell_start;   // size nx*ny+1\n    std::vector<int> items;        // point indices, ascending within each cell\n    int live_at_build = 0;         // number of points present at last rebuild\n\n    void configure(const double threshold) {\n        // Keep the number of cells bounded for very small thresholds; the\n        // search radius compensates for cells larger than the threshold.\n        cell = std::max(threshold, MAX_WIDTH / 512.0);\n        nx = static_cast<int>(MAX_WIDTH / cell) + 1;\n        ny = static_cast<int>(MAX_HEIGHT / cell) + 1;\n        reach = static_cast<int>(std::ceil(threshold / cell));\n        if (reach < 1) reach = 1;\n        cell_start.assign(static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny + 1, 0);\n    }\n\n    inline int cellX(const Point& p) const {\n        int c = static_cast<int>(p.x / cell);\n        return std::min(std::max(c, 0), nx - 1);\n    }\n    inline int cellY(const Point& p) const {\n        int c = static_cast<int>(p.y / cell);\n        return std::min(std::max(c, 0), ny - 1);\n    }\n\n    // Counting sort of the given point indices into the grid cells.\n    void build(const std::vector<Point>& points, const std::vector<int>& live) {\n        const size_t ncells = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;\n        std::fill(cell_start.begin(), cell_start.end(), 0);\n        for (const int idx : live) {\n            const size_t c = static_cast<size_t>(cellY(points[idx])) * nx + cellX(points[idx]);\n            ++cell_start[c + 1];\n        }\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < ncells; ++c) {\n            cell_start[c + 1] += cell_start[c];\n        }\n        items.resize(live.size());\n        std::vector<int> cursor(cell_start.begin(), cell_start.end() - 1);\n        for (const int idx : live) {\n            const size_t c = static_cast<size_t>(cellY(points[idx])) * nx + cellX(points[idx]);\n            items[cursor[c]++] = idx;\n        }\n        live_at_build = static_cast<int>(live.size());\n    }\n};\n\n// Scratch buffers for growing a candidate cluster (reused across seeds).\nstruct ClusterWorkspace {\n    std::vector<int> idx;      // candidate point indices\n    std::vector<double> px;    // candidate coordinates (kept next to the index\n    std::vector<double> py;    // arrays so the update loop can vectorize)\n    std::vector<double> maxd;  // running max distance to the current members\n    std::vector<int> members;\n\n    void reserve(const int n) {\n        idx.reserve(n);\n        px.reserve(n);\n        py.reserve(n);\n        maxd.reserve(n);\n        members.reserve(n);\n    }\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point.\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster; the members are left in\n// work.members.\nstatic int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                                    const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                                    const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                    const double threshold,\n                                    const NeighborGrid& grid,\n                                    ClusterWorkspace& work) {\n    std::vector<int>& idx = work.idx;\n    std::vector<double>& px = work.px;\n    std::vector<double>& py = work.py;\n    std::vector<double>& maxd = work.maxd;\n    std::vector<int>& members = work.members;\n\n    idx.clear();\n    px.clear();\n    py.clear();\n    maxd.clear();\n    members.clear();\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    const Point seed = points[seed_point];\n\n    // Collect all unclustered points closer than the threshold to the seed.\n    const int cx = grid.cellX(seed);\n    const int cy = grid.cellY(seed);\n    const int x0 = std::max(cx - grid.reach, 0);\n    const int x1 = std::min(cx + grid.reach, grid.nx - 1);\n    const int y0 = std::max(cy - grid.reach, 0);\n    const int y1 = std::min(cy + grid.reach, grid.ny - 1);\n\n    int best = -1;\n    int best_idx = 0;\n    double best_d = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n\n    for (int gy = y0; gy <= y1; ++gy) {\n        const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(gy) * grid.nx;\n        const int begin = grid.cell_start[row + x0];\n        const int end = grid.cell_start[row + x1 + 1];\n        for (int k = begin; k < end; ++k) {\n            const int c = grid.items[k];\n            if (c == seed_point || clustered[c]) continue;\n            const double d = distance(points[c], seed);\n            if (d >= threshold) continue;\n            if (d < best_d || (d == best_d && c < best_idx)) {\n                best = static_cast<int>(idx.size());\n                best_idx = c;\n                best_d = d;\n            }\n            idx.push_back(c);\n            px.push_back(points[c].x);\n            py.push_back(points[c].y);\n            maxd.push_back(d);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Iteratively add the candidate with the smallest cluster diameter.\n    while (best >= 0) {\n        const int added = idx[best];\n        members.push_back(added);\n\n        const double ax = px[best];\n        const double ay = py[best];\n        const int n = static_cast<int>(idx.size());\n\n        // Update the running maxima (branch free, vectorizable).\n        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            const double dx = px[i] - ax;\n            const double dy = py[i] - ay;\n            const double d = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n            maxd[i] = maxd[i] > d ? maxd[i] : d;\n        }\n\n        // Compact out the candidates that can no longer join and pick the next\n        // best one. The newly added member is dropped as well.\n        int out = 0;\n        int nbest = -1;\n        int nbest_idx = 0;\n        double nbest_d = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            if (i == best || maxd[i] >= threshold) continue;\n            const double d = maxd[i];\n            const int c = idx[i];\n            if (d < nbest_d || (d == nbest_d && c < nbest_idx)) {\n                nbest = out;\n                nbest_idx = c;\n                nbest_d = d;\n            }\n            idx[out] = c;\n            px[out] = px[i];\n            py[out] = py[i];\n            maxd[out] = d;\n            ++out;\n        }\n        idx.resize(out);\n        px.resize(out);\n        py.resize(out);\n        maxd.resize(out);\n        best = nbest;\n        best_idx = nbest_idx;\n        best_d = nbest_d;\n    }\n\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (distributed over MPI_COMM_WORLD)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold,\n                                  const int rank,\n                                  const int nranks) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    NeighborGrid grid;\n    grid.configure(threshold);\n    grid.build(points, unclustered_indices);\n\n    ClusterWorkspace work;\n    work.reserve(N);\n\n    std::vector<int> bcast_members(N);\n\n    struct { int card; int seed; } local, global;\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int remaining = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n        // Rebuild the grid once a significant fraction of its points has been\n        // clustered, so that the neighborhood scans stay cheap.\n        if (remaining * 4 <= grid.live_at_build * 3) {\n            grid.build(points, unclustered_indices);\n        }\n\n        local.card = -1;\n        local.seed = N;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n\n        // Try each unclustered point assigned to this rank as a seed.\n        // Round-robin over the (index sorted) unclustered points balances the\n        // very different per-seed costs well and visits the local seeds in\n        // ascending order, which matches the sequential tie-breaking.\n        for (int pos = rank; pos < remaining; pos += nranks) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[pos];\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points,\n                                                             threshold, grid, work);\n            if (cardinality > local.card) {\n                local.card = cardinality;\n                local.seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = work.members;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // MPI_MAXLOC keeps the largest cardinality and, on ties, the smallest\n        // point index - exactly the sequential selection rule.\n        MPI_Allreduce(&local, &global, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (global.seed >= N || global.card <= 0) {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n\n        // The winning seed's owner is determined from its position in the\n        // (globally replicated, ascending) list of unclustered points.\n        const int owner = static_cast<int>(\n            std::lower_bound(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                             global.seed) - unclustered_indices.begin()) % nranks;\n\n        if (rank == owner) {\n            std::copy(best_cluster_members.begin(), best_cluster_members.end(),\n                      bcast_members.begin());\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(bcast_members.data(), global.card, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = global.seed;\n        cluster.members.assign(bcast_members.begin(), bcast_members.begin() + global.card);\n\n        // Mark all members as clustered\n        for (const int m : cluster.members) {\n            clustered[m] = true;\n        }\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n        // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n        unclustered_indices.erase(\n            std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                          [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n            unclustered_indices.end()\n        );\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default:          return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n\n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        if (rank == 0) {\n            printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n    \n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n    \n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n        \n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n        \n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n            \n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n            \n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Find the closest unclustered point to the current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n// Returns -1 if no such point exists\nint findClosestPoint(const std::vector<int>& cluster_members,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                     const std::vector<bool>& in_cluster,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold,\n                     const int point_count) {\n    int closest_point = -1;\n    double min_diameter = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n    \n    // Try each unclustered point as a candidate\n    for (int candidate = 0; candidate < point_count; ++candidate) {\n        // Skip if already clustered or already in this cluster\n        if (clustered[candidate] || in_cluster[candidate]) continue;\n        \n        // Calculate the maximum distance from candidate to all cluster members\n        double max_dist = 0.0;\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = cluster_members[i];\n            const double dist = distance(points[candidate], points[member]);\n            max_dist = std::max(max_dist, dist);\n        }\n        \n        // If adding this point keeps diameter below threshold and is better than current best\n        if (max_dist < threshold && max_dist < min_diameter) {\n            min_diameter = max_dist;\n            closest_point = candidate;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return closest_point;\n}\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster\nint generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                              const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                              const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                              const double threshold,\n                              const int point_count,\n                              std::vector<int>* cluster_members = nullptr) {\n    std::vector<bool> in_cluster(point_count, false);\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    \n    // Add seed point\n    in_cluster[seed_point] = true;\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n    \n    // Iteratively add closest points\n    while (static_cast<int>(members.size()) < point_count) {\n        // Find closest point to current cluster that maintains diameter < threshold\n        const int closest = findClosestPoint(members, clustered, in_cluster, points, \n                                             threshold, point_count);\n        \n        if (closest < 0) break; // No more points can be added\n        \n        in_cluster[closest] = true;\n        members.push_back(closest);\n    }\n    \n    // Copy members if requested\n    if (cluster_members) {\n        *cluster_members = members;\n    }\n    \n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n    \n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n    \n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        int max_cardinality = -1;\n        int best_seed = -1;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n        \n        // Try each unclustered point as a seed\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < unclustered_indices.size(); ++i) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[i];\n            if (clustered[seed]) continue;\n            \n            std::vector<int> candidate_members;\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points, \n                                                       threshold, N, \n                                                       &candidate_members);\n            \n            if (cardinality > max_cardinality) {\n                max_cardinality = cardinality;\n                best_seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = candidate_members;\n            }\n        }\n        \n        // If we found a cluster, add it\n        if (best_seed >= 0 && max_cardinality > 0) {\n            Cluster cluster;\n            cluster.seed_point = best_seed;\n            cluster.members = best_cluster_members;\n            clusters.push_back(cluster);\n            \n            // Mark all members as clustered\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < best_cluster_members.size(); ++i) {\n                clustered[best_cluster_members[i]] = true;\n            }\n            \n            // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n            unclustered_indices.erase(\n                std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                              [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n                unclustered_indices.end()\n            );\n        } else {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n    \n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n    \n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n        \n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]], \n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n        \n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\", \n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n        \n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\", \n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n    \n    return valid;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Number of points (default: external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int num_points = 1000;\n    double threshold = 2.0;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            num_points = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-t\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            threshold = atof(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    if (num_points <= 0 || threshold <= 0.0) {\n        printf(\"Error: Invalid parameters (num_points=%d, threshold=%.2f)\\n\", \n               num_points, threshold);\n        return 1;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"QT Clustering Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"Number of points: %d\\n\", num_points);\n    printf(\"Distance threshold: %.2f\\n\", threshold);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();\n    auto cluster_time = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(\n        cluster_end - cluster_start);\n    \n    printf(\"Clustering time: MPI Distributed Memory Version\n//\n// QT (Quality Threshold) clustering is an algorithm that builds clusters\n// by starting with a seed point and iteratively adding the closest point\n// that maintains the cluster's diameter below a threshold.\n//\n// Parallelization strategy:\n//   * Every rank redundantly generates the (deterministic) input data, so no\n//     input distribution is required.\n//   * In each round of the outer loop, the candidate clusters of the remaining\n//     unclustered seed points are distributed round-robin over the ranks.\n//     Each rank grows the candidate clusters of its own seeds independently.\n//   * A single MPI_Allreduce(MPI_MAXLOC) selects the globally best seed, using\n//     the same tie-breaking rule as the sequential code (largest cardinality,\n//     smallest point index on ties).\n//   * The owner of the winning seed broadcasts the winning cluster members, so\n//     all ranks keep an identical view of the clustering state and no final\n//     gather is required.\n//\n// Serial optimizations that preserve the exact results of the original code:\n//   * Incremental candidate diameters: instead of recomputing the maximum\n//     distance from a candidate to every cluster member, each candidate keeps\n//     a running maximum which is updated with the distance to the newly added\n//     member only (max over a set is order independent, hence bit-identical).\n//   * Candidates whose running maximum reached the threshold are dropped\n//     permanently (the maximum can only grow).\n//   * A uniform grid restricts the initial candidate set of a seed to the\n//     points that are closer than the threshold to that seed - all other\n//     points can never join the cluster since the seed is a cluster member.\n\n#include <algorithm>\n#include <chrono>\n#include <cmath>\n#include <cstdio>\n#include <cstdlib>\n#include <cstring>\n#include <limits>\n#include <vector>\n\n#include <mpi.h>\n\n#include \"../common/results_output.hpp\"\n\nstatic const double MAX_WIDTH = 20.0;\nstatic const double MAX_HEIGHT = 20.0;\n\n// Structure to represent a point in 2D space\nstruct Point {\n    double x, y;\n};\n\n// Structure to represent a cluster\nstruct Cluster {\n    std::vector<int> members;\n    int seed_point;\n};\n\n// Generate synthetic 2D point data in clusters\nvoid generateSyntheticData(std::vector<Point>& points, const int N, unsigned int seed = 42) {\n    auto frand = [&seed]() mutable { return rand_r(&seed) / static_cast<double>(RAND_MAX); };\n\n    const double min_dim = std::min(MAX_WIDTH, MAX_HEIGHT);\n    int count = 0;\n\n    while (count < N) {\n        // Create group_cnt points within a circle of radius R\n        // around center point (cntr_x, cntr_y)\n        const double cntr_x = frand() * MAX_WIDTH;\n        const double cntr_y = frand() * MAX_HEIGHT;\n        const double R = frand() * min_dim / 2.0;\n        int group_cnt = static_cast<int>(frand() * (N / 30.0));\n\n        // Make sure we don't make more points than we need\n        if (group_cnt > (N - count)) {\n            group_cnt = N - count;\n        }\n\n        while (group_cnt > 0) {\n            const double sign = (frand() < 0.5) ? -1.0 : 1.0;\n            const double r = frand() * R;\n            const double dx = (2.0 * frand() - 1.0) * r;\n            const double dy = std::sqrt(r * r - dx * dx) * sign;\n            const double x = cntr_x + dx;\n            const double y = cntr_y + dy;\n\n            if (x < 0 || x > MAX_WIDTH || y < 0 || y > MAX_HEIGHT) {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            points[count] = {x, y};\n            count++;\n            group_cnt--;\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// Calculate Euclidean distance between two points\ninline double distance(const Point& p1, const Point& p2) {\n    double dx = p1.x - p2.x;\n    double dy = p1.y - p2.y;\n    return std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n}\n\n// Uniform bucket grid over the still unclustered points. It is used to find,\n// for a given seed, all points that are closer than the threshold - only those\n// can ever become members of the seed's candidate cluster.\nstruct NeighborGrid {\n    double cell = 1.0;\n    int nx = 1, ny = 1;\n    int reach = 1;                 // number of cells to search in each direction\n    std::vector<int> cell_start;   // size nx*ny+1\n    std::vector<int> items;        // point indices, ascending within each cell\n    int live_at_build = 0;         // number of points present at last rebuild\n\n    void configure(const double threshold) {\n        // Keep the number of cells bounded for very small thresholds; the\n        // search radius compensates for cells larger than the threshold.\n        cell = std::max(threshold, MAX_WIDTH / 512.0);\n        nx = static_cast<int>(MAX_WIDTH / cell) + 1;\n        ny = static_cast<int>(MAX_HEIGHT / cell) + 1;\n        reach = static_cast<int>(std::ceil(threshold / cell));\n        if (reach < 1) reach = 1;\n        cell_start.assign(static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny + 1, 0);\n    }\n\n    inline int cellX(const Point& p) const {\n        int c = static_cast<int>(p.x / cell);\n        return std::min(std::max(c, 0), nx - 1);\n    }\n    inline int cellY(const Point& p) const {\n        int c = static_cast<int>(p.y / cell);\n        return std::min(std::max(c, 0), ny - 1);\n    }\n\n    // Counting sort of the given point indices into the grid cells.\n    void build(const std::vector<Point>& points, const std::vector<int>& live) {\n        const size_t ncells = static_cast<size_t>(nx) * ny;\n        std::fill(cell_start.begin(), cell_start.end(), 0);\n        for (const int idx : live) {\n            const size_t c = static_cast<size_t>(cellY(points[idx])) * nx + cellX(points[idx]);\n            ++cell_start[c + 1];\n        }\n        for (size_t c = 0; c < ncells; ++c) {\n            cell_start[c + 1] += cell_start[c];\n        }\n        items.resize(live.size());\n        std::vector<int> cursor(cell_start.begin(), cell_start.end() - 1);\n        for (const int idx : live) {\n            const size_t c = static_cast<size_t>(cellY(points[idx])) * nx + cellX(points[idx]);\n            items[cursor[c]++] = idx;\n        }\n        live_at_build = static_cast<int>(live.size());\n    }\n};\n\n// Scratch buffers for growing a candidate cluster (reused across seeds).\nstruct ClusterWorkspace {\n    std::vector<int> idx;      // candidate point indices\n    std::vector<double> px;    // candidate coordinates (kept next to the index\n    std::vector<double> py;    // arrays so the update loop can vectorize)\n    std::vector<double> maxd;  // running max distance to the current members\n    std::vector<int> members;\n\n    void reserve(const int n) {\n        idx.reserve(n);\n        px.reserve(n);\n        py.reserve(n);\n        maxd.reserve(n);\n        members.reserve(n);\n    }\n};\n\n// Generate a candidate cluster starting from a seed point.\n// Returns the cardinality (size) of the cluster; the members are left in\n// work.members.\nstatic int generateCandidateCluster(const int seed_point,\n                                    const std::vector<bool>& clustered,\n                                    const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                    const double threshold,\n                                    const NeighborGrid& grid,\n                                    ClusterWorkspace& work) {\n    std::vector<int>& idx = work.idx;\n    std::vector<double>& px = work.px;\n    std::vector<double>& py = work.py;\n    std::vector<double>& maxd = work.maxd;\n    std::vector<int>& members = work.members;\n\n    idx.clear();\n    px.clear();\n    py.clear();\n    maxd.clear();\n    members.clear();\n    members.push_back(seed_point);\n\n    const Point seed = points[seed_point];\n\n    // Collect all unclustered points closer than the threshold to the seed.\n    const int cx = grid.cellX(seed);\n    const int cy = grid.cellY(seed);\n    const int x0 = std::max(cx - grid.reach, 0);\n    const int x1 = std::min(cx + grid.reach, grid.nx - 1);\n    const int y0 = std::max(cy - grid.reach, 0);\n    const int y1 = std::min(cy + grid.reach, grid.ny - 1);\n\n    int best = -1;\n    int best_idx = 0;\n    double best_d = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n\n    for (int gy = y0; gy <= y1; ++gy) {\n        const size_t row = static_cast<size_t>(gy) * grid.nx;\n        const int begin = grid.cell_start[row + x0];\n        const int end = grid.cell_start[row + x1 + 1];\n        for (int k = begin; k < end; ++k) {\n            const int c = grid.items[k];\n            if (c == seed_point || clustered[c]) continue;\n            const double d = distance(points[c], seed);\n            if (d >= threshold) continue;\n            if (d < best_d || (d == best_d && c < best_idx)) {\n                best = static_cast<int>(idx.size());\n                best_idx = c;\n                best_d = d;\n            }\n            idx.push_back(c);\n            px.push_back(points[c].x);\n            py.push_back(points[c].y);\n            maxd.push_back(d);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Iteratively add the candidate with the smallest cluster diameter.\n    while (best >= 0) {\n        const int added = idx[best];\n        members.push_back(added);\n\n        const double ax = px[best];\n        const double ay = py[best];\n        const int n = static_cast<int>(idx.size());\n\n        // Update the running maxima (branch free, vectorizable).\n        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            const double dx = px[i] - ax;\n            const double dy = py[i] - ay;\n            const double d = std::sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy);\n            maxd[i] = maxd[i] > d ? maxd[i] : d;\n        }\n\n        // Compact out the candidates that can no longer join and pick the next\n        // best one. The newly added member is dropped as well.\n        int out = 0;\n        int nbest = -1;\n        int nbest_idx = 0;\n        double nbest_d = std::numeric_limits<double>::max();\n        for (int i = 0; i < n; ++i) {\n            if (i == best || maxd[i] >= threshold) continue;\n            const double d = maxd[i];\n            const int c = idx[i];\n            if (d < nbest_d || (d == nbest_d && c < nbest_idx)) {\n                nbest = out;\n                nbest_idx = c;\n                nbest_d = d;\n            }\n            idx[out] = c;\n            px[out] = px[i];\n            py[out] = py[i];\n            maxd[out] = d;\n            ++out;\n        }\n        idx.resize(out);\n        px.resize(out);\n        py.resize(out);\n        maxd.resize(out);\n        best = nbest;\n        best_idx = nbest_idx;\n        best_d = nbest_d;\n    }\n\n    return static_cast<int>(members.size());\n}\n\n// Main QT clustering algorithm (distributed over MPI_COMM_WORLD)\nstd::vector<Cluster> qtClustering(const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                                  const double threshold,\n                                  const int rank,\n                                  const int nranks) {\n    const int N = static_cast<int>(points.size());\n    std::vector<bool> clustered(N, false);\n    std::vector<int> unclustered_indices;\n    std::vector<Cluster> clusters;\n\n    // Initialize unclustered indices\n    unclustered_indices.reserve(N);\n    for (int i = 0; i < N; ++i) {\n        unclustered_indices.push_back(i);\n    }\n\n    NeighborGrid grid;\n    grid.configure(threshold);\n    grid.build(points, unclustered_indices);\n\n    ClusterWorkspace work;\n    work.reserve(N);\n\n    std::vector<int> bcast_members(N);\n\n    struct { int card; int seed; } local, global;\n\n    // Main clustering loop\n    while (!unclustered_indices.empty()) {\n        const int remaining = static_cast<int>(unclustered_indices.size());\n\n        // Rebuild the grid once a significant fraction of its points has been\n        // clustered, so that the neighborhood scans stay cheap.\n        if (remaining * 4 <= grid.live_at_build * 3) {\n            grid.build(points, unclustered_indices);\n        }\n\n        local.card = -1;\n        local.seed = N;\n        std::vector<int> best_cluster_members;\n\n        // Try each unclustered point assigned to this rank as a seed.\n        // Round-robin over the (index sorted) unclustered points balances the\n        // very different per-seed costs well and visits the local seeds in\n        // ascending order, which matches the sequential tie-breaking.\n        for (int pos = rank; pos < remaining; pos += nranks) {\n            const int seed = unclustered_indices[pos];\n            const int cardinality = generateCandidateCluster(seed, clustered, points,\n                                                             threshold, grid, work);\n            if (cardinality > local.card) {\n                local.card = cardinality;\n                local.seed = seed;\n                best_cluster_members = work.members;\n            }\n        }\n\n        // MPI_MAXLOC keeps the largest cardinality and, on ties, the smallest\n        // point index - exactly the sequential selection rule.\n        MPI_Allreduce(&local, &global, 1, MPI_2INT, MPI_MAXLOC, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        if (global.seed >= N || global.card <= 0) {\n            // No more clusters can be formed\n            break;\n        }\n\n        // The winning seed's owner is determined from its position in the\n        // (globally replicated, ascending) list of unclustered points.\n        const int owner = static_cast<int>(\n            std::lower_bound(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                             global.seed) - unclustered_indices.begin()) % nranks;\n\n        if (rank == owner) {\n            std::copy(best_cluster_members.begin(), best_cluster_members.end(),\n                      bcast_members.begin());\n        }\n        MPI_Bcast(bcast_members.data(), global.card, MPI_INT, owner, MPI_COMM_WORLD);\n\n        Cluster cluster;\n        cluster.seed_point = global.seed;\n        cluster.members.assign(bcast_members.begin(), bcast_members.begin() + global.card);\n\n        // Mark all members as clustered\n        for (const int m : cluster.members) {\n            clustered[m] = true;\n        }\n        clusters.push_back(std::move(cluster));\n\n        // Remove clustered points from unclustered list\n        unclustered_indices.erase(\n            std::remove_if(unclustered_indices.begin(), unclustered_indices.end(),\n                          [&clustered](int idx) { return clustered[idx]; }),\n            unclustered_indices.end()\n        );\n    }\n\n    return clusters;\n}\n\n// Validation: check that clusters satisfy the QT clustering properties\nbool validateClusters(const std::vector<Cluster>& clusters,\n                     const std::vector<Point>& points,\n                     const double threshold) {\n    bool valid = true;\n\n    printf(\"Validating clusters:\\n\");\n\n    // Check each cluster\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        const auto& cluster = clusters[c];\n        double max_diameter = 0.0;\n\n        // Check diameter (max distance between any two points)\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < cluster.members.size(); ++i) {\n            for (size_t j = i + 1; j < cluster.members.size(); ++j) {\n                const double dist = distance(points[cluster.members[i]],\n                                           points[cluster.members[j]]);\n                max_diameter = std::max(max_diameter, dist);\n            }\n        }\n\n        if (c < 10) { // Print first 10 clusters\n            printf(\"  Cluster %zu: size=%zu, seed=%d, diameter=%.4f\\n\",\n                   c, cluster.members.size(), cluster.seed_point, max_diameter);\n        }\n\n        // Validate diameter is within threshold\n        if (max_diameter > threshold * 1.001) { // Allow small numerical error\n            printf(\"ERROR: Cluster %zu has diameter %.4f > threshold %.4f\\n\",\n                   c, max_diameter, threshold);\n            valid = false;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Check for duplicate memberships\n    std::vector<int> membership(points.size(), -1);\n    for (size_t c = 0; c < clusters.size(); ++c) {\n        for (size_t i = 0; i < clusters[c].members.size(); ++i) {\n            const int member = clusters[c].members[i];\n            if (membership[member] >= 0) {\n                printf(\"ERROR: Point %d appears in multiple clusters (%d and %zu)\\n\",\n                       member, membership[member], c);\n                valid = false;\n            }\n            membership[member] = static_cast<int>(c);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Count clustered points\n    int clustered_count = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < membership.size(); ++i) {\n        if (membership[i] >= 0) clustered_count++;\n    }\n\n    printf(\"Total points: %zu, Clustered: %d, Unclustered: %zu\\n\",\n           points.size(), clustered_count, points.size() - clustered_count);\n\n    return
